```css
   РАЗДЕЛИТЕЛЬ С ЦИТАТОЙ
   ============================================ */
.quote-separator {
    padding: var(--section-spacing) 0;
    background-color: var(--color-black);
    color: var(--color-white);
}

.quote-text {
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: clamp(1.5rem, 4vw, 3rem);
    font-weight: 400;
    line-height: 1.4;
    font-style: italic;
    max-width: 900px;
    margin: 0 auto;
    text-align: center;
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(30px);
}

.quote-author {
    display: block;
    font-family: var(--font-sans);
    font-size: 1rem;
    font-weight: 500;
    font-style: normal;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.15em;
    margin-top: 2rem;
    text-align: center;
    opacity: 0.7;
}

/* ============================================
   СЕТКА ЛИЧНОСТЕЙ / PERSONALITIES GRID
   ============================================ */
.personalities {
    padding: var(--section-spacing) 0;
    background-color: var(--color-white);
}

.personalities-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(320px, 1fr));
    gap: 2rem;
    margin-top: 4rem;
}

/* ============================================
   КАРТОЧКА ЛИЧНОСТИ
   ============================================
   КАК ИЗМЕНИТЬ КАРТОЧКУ:
   - .personality-card-image-placeholder — замените на <img src="путь/к/изображению.jpg">
   - .personality-card-name — имя личности
   - .personality-card-country — страна
   - .personality-card-years — годы жизни
   - .personality-card-description — краткое описание
   - .personality-card-tag — тег/категория
   ============================================ */
.personality-card {
    position: relative;
    background-color: var(--color-white);
    border: 1px solid var(--color-gray-light);
    overflow: hidden;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: all 0.4s var(--ease-smooth);
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(30px);
}

.personality-card:hover {
    transform: translateY(-5px);
    box-shadow: 0 20px 60px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
    border-color: var(--color-black);
}

.personality-card-image {
    width: 100%;
    height: 400px;
    background-color: var(--color-gray-light);
    position: relative;
    overflow: hidden;
}

.personality-card-placeholder {
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: 1.5rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    color: var(--color-gray);
    background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--color-gray-light) 0%, var(--color-ivory) 100%);
}

/* Стили для изображений в карточках */
.personality-card-image img {
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    object-fit: cover;
    transition: transform 0.6s var(--ease-smooth);
}

.personality-card:hover .personality-card-image img {
    transform: scale(1.1);
}

.personality-card-content {
    padding: 2rem;
}

.personality-card-meta {
    display: flex;
    gap: 1rem;
    margin-bottom: 1rem;
    font-size: 0.75rem;
    font-weight: 500;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.1em;
    color: var(--color-gray);
}

.personality-card-name {
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: 1.75rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    margin-bottom: 0.75rem;
    color: var(--color-black);
}

.personality-card-description {
    font-size: 1rem;
    line-height: 1.6;
    color: var(--color-gray-dark);
    margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
}

.personality-card-tag {
    display: inline-block;
    font-size: 0.75rem;
    font-weight: 500;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.1em;
    padding: 0.5rem 1rem;
    background-color: var(--color-black);
    color: var(--color-white);
}

/* ============================================
   МОДАЛЬНОЕ ОКНО ДЕТАЛЕЙ
   ============================================ */
.personality-modal {
    position: fixed;
    top: 0;
    left: 0;
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    background-color: rgba(10, 10, 10, 0.95);
    z-index: 1000;
    display: none;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    padding: 2rem;
    overflow-y: auto;
    backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
}

.personality-modal.active {
    display: flex;
}

.modal-close {
    position: fixed;
    top: 2rem;
    right: 2rem;
    width: 48px;
    height: 48px;
    background-color: transparent;
    border: 1px solid var(--color-white);
    color: var(--color-white);
    cursor: pointer;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    transition: all 0.3s var(--ease-smooth);
    z-index: 1001;
}

.modal-close:hover {
    background-color: var(--color-white);
    color: var(--color-black);
}

.modal-content {
    max-width: 1200px;
    width: 100%;
    max-height: 90vh;
    overflow-y: auto;
    padding: 2rem 0;
}

/* ============================================
   ДЕТАЛЬНЫЙ ПРОФИЛЬ ЛИЧНОСТИ
   ============================================
   КАК ИЗМЕНИТЬ ПРОФИЛЬ:
   - .personality-detail-image-placeholder — замените на <img>
   - .personality-detail-name — имя
   - .personality-detail-country — страна
   - .personality-detail-years — годы
   - .personality-detail-section — биография, инновации, влияние
   - .personality-detail-gallery — галерея работ
   ============================================ */
.personality-detail {
    background-color: var(--color-white);
    color: var(--color-black);
    border-radius: 0;
}

.personality-detail-hero {
    width: 100%;
    height: 50vh;
    min-height: 400px;
    background-color: var(--color-gray-light);
    margin-bottom: 4rem;
    position: relative;
    overflow: hidden;
    border-radius: 0;
}

.personality-detail-image-placeholder {
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: 3rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    color: var(--color-gray);
    background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--color-gray-light) 0%, var(--color-ivory) 100%);
}

/* Стили для изображений в детальных профилях */
.personality-detail-hero img {
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    object-fit: cover;
}

.personality-detail-content {
    padding: 0 2rem 4rem;
}

.personality-detail-header {
    margin-bottom: 4rem;
    padding-bottom: 2.5rem;
    border-bottom: 2px solid var(--color-gray-light);
    position: relative;
}

.personality-detail-header::after {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    bottom: -2px;
    left: 0;
    width: 100px;
    height: 2px;
    background-color: var(--color-black);
}

.personality-detail-meta {
    display: flex;
    gap: 1.5rem;
    margin-bottom: 1rem;
    font-size: 0.875rem;
    font-weight: 500;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.1em;
    color: var(--color-gray);
}

.personality-detail-name {
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: clamp(2.5rem, 6vw, 4.5rem);
    font-weight: 900;
    line-height: 1.1;
    color: var(--color-black);
}

.personality-detail-body {
    max-width: 800px;
}

.personality-detail-section {
    margin-bottom: 4rem;
    padding: 2.5rem;
    background-color: var(--color-ivory);
    border: 1px solid var(--color-gray-light);
    transition: all 0.3s var(--ease-smooth);
    position: relative;
}

.personality-detail-section:hover {
    border-color: var(--color-black);
    box-shadow: 0 10px 40px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.05);
    transform: translateY(-2px);
}

.personality-detail-section::before {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    top: 0;
    left: 0;
    width: 4px;
    height: 100%;
    background-color: var(--color-black);
    opacity: 0;
    transition: opacity 0.3s var(--ease-smooth);
}

.personality-detail-section:hover::before {
    opacity: 1;
}

.personality-detail-section h3 {
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: 1.75rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
    color: var(--color-black);
}

.personality-detail-section p {
    font-size: 1.125rem;
    line-height: 1.8;
    color: var(--color-gray-dark);
    margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
}

.personality-detail-section ul {
    list-style: none;
    padding: 0;
}

.personality-detail-section li {
    font-size: 1.125rem;
    line-height: 1.8;
    color: var(--color-gray-dark);
    padding-left: 2rem;
    position: relative;
    margin-bottom: 1rem;
}

.personality-detail-section li::before {
    content: '—';
    position: absolute;
    left: 0;
    color: var(--color-black);
    font-weight: 700;
}

.personality-detail-gallery {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(250px, 1fr));
    gap: 2rem;
    margin-top: 4rem;
    padding-top: 4rem;
    border-top: 2px solid var(--color-gray-light);
    position: relative;
}

.personality-detail-gallery::before {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    top: -2px;
    left: 0;
    width: 100px;
    height: 2px;
    background-color: var(--color-black);
}

.gallery-item {
    width: 100%;
    height: 300px;
    background-color: var(--color-gray-light);
    position: relative;
    overflow: hidden;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: transform 0.3s var(--ease-smooth);
}

.gallery-item:hover {
    transform: scale(1.05);
}

.gallery-placeholder {
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    font-family: var(--font-sans);
    font-size: 0.875rem;
    font-weight: 500;
    color: var(--color-gray);
    background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--color-gray-light) 0%, var(--color-ivory) 100%);
}

/* Стили для изображений в галерее */
.gallery-item img {
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    object-fit: cover;
    transition: transform 0.4s var(--ease-smooth);
}

.gallery-item:hover img {
    transform: scale(1.05);
}

/* ============================================
   ТЕМЫ И ВЛИЯНИЕ / THEMES SECTION
   ============================================ */
.themes {
    padding: var(--section-spacing) 0;
    background-color: var(--color-ivory);
}

.themes-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(300px, 1fr));
    gap: 2rem;
    margin-top: 4rem;
}

.theme-card {
    background-color: var(--color-white);
    padding: 3rem;
    border: 1px solid var(--color-gray-light);
    transition: all 0.4s var(--ease-smooth);
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(30px);
}

.theme-card:hover {
    transform: translateY(-5px);
    box-shadow: 0 20px 60px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
    border-color: var(--color-black);
}

.theme-title {
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: 1.5rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
    color: var(--color-black);
}

.theme-description {
    font-size: 1rem;
    line-height: 1.7;
    color: var(--color-gray-dark);
}

/* ============================================
   ЗАКЛЮЧЕНИЕ / CLOSING SECTION
   ============================================ */
.closing {
    padding: var(--section-spacing) 0;
    background-color: var(--color-white);
}

.closing-content {
    max-width: 900px;
    margin: 0 auto;
    text-align: center;
}

.closing-title {
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: clamp(2.5rem, 6vw, 4.5rem);
    font-weight: 900;
    margin-bottom: 3rem;
    color: var(--color-black);
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(30px);
}

.closing-text {
    font-size: 1.25rem;
    line-height: 1.8;
    color: var(--color-gray-dark);
}

.closing-text p {
    margin-bottom: 2rem;
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(30px);
}

.closing-text p:last-child {
    margin-bottom: 0;
}

/* ============================================
   ФУТЕР / FOOTER
   ============================================ */
.footer {
    padding: 4rem 0;
    background-color: var(--color-black);
    color: var(--color-white);
    text-align: center;
}

.footer-content {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 1rem;
}

.footer-text {
    font-size: 0.875rem;
    font-weight: 400;
    letter-spacing: 0.1em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    opacity: 0.7;
}

.footer-year {
    font-size: 0.75rem;
    font-weight: 300;
    opacity: 0.5;
}

/* ============================================
   АДАПТИВНОСТЬ / RESPONSIVE
   ============================================ */
@media (max-width: 1024px) {
    :root {
        --container-padding: 4vw;
        --section-spacing: 10vh;
    }
    
    .personalities-grid {
        grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(280px, 1fr));
        gap: 1.5rem;
    }
    
    .manifesto-content {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
        gap: 3rem;
    }
}

@media (max-width: 768px) {
    :root {
        --container-padding: 5vw;
        --section-spacing: 8vh;
    }
    
    .hero {
        min-height: 500px;
    }
    
    .personalities-grid {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }
    
    .themes-grid {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }
    
    .timeline-track {
        padding-left: 2rem;
    }
    
    .timeline-item::before {
        left: -2.5rem;
    }
    
    .personality-detail-hero {
        height: 40vh;
        min-height: 300px;
    }
    
    .personality-detail-content {
        padding: 0 1rem;
    }
    
    .modal-close {
        top: 1rem;
        right: 1rem;
    }
}

@media (max-width: 480px) {
    .hero-title {
        font-size: 3rem;
    }
    
    .section-title {
        font-size: 2rem;
    }
    
    .personality-card-image {
        height: 300px;
    }
}

/* ============================================
   УТИЛИТЫ ДЛЯ АНИМАЦИЙ
   ============================================ */
.fade-in {
    opacity: 1 !important;
    transform: translateY(0) !important;
}

/* ============================================
   ПОДДЕРЖКА REDUCED MOTION
   ============================================ */
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
    *,
    *::before,
    *::after {
        animation-duration: 0.01ms !important;
        animation-iteration-count: 1 !important;
        transition-duration: 0.01ms !important;
    }
    
    .hero-scroll-hint {
        animation: none;
    }
}


```

```css
/* ============================================
   ПРЕМИАЛЬНАЯ ВЫСТАВКА: АВАНГАРД В МОДЕ
   ============================================
   Стили для цифровой выставки об авангарде
   в искусстве и моде конца XIX–XX века
   ============================================ */

/* ============================================
   БАЗОВЫЕ СТИЛИ И СБРОС
   ============================================ */
* {
    margin: 0;
    padding: 0;
    box-sizing: border-box;
}

:root {
    /* Цветовая палитра */
    --color-black: #0a0a0a;
    --color-white: #fafafa;
    --color-ivory: #f5f5f0;
    --color-gray-light: #e8e8e3;
    --color-gray: #8a8a85;
    --color-gray-dark: #3a3a35;
    --color-accent: #1a1a1a;
    
    /* Типографика */
    --font-serif: 'Playfair Display', serif;
    --font-sans: 'Inter', sans-serif;
    
    /* Размеры */
    --container-max-width: 1400px;
    --container-padding: 5vw;
    --section-spacing: 15vh;
    
    /* Анимации */
    --ease-smooth: cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1);
    --ease-out-expo: cubic-bezier(0.19, 1, 0.22, 1);
}

html {
    font-size: 16px;
    /* scroll-behavior удалён для полностью нативной прокрутки без задержек */
}

body {
    font-family: var(--font-sans);
    font-weight: 400;
    line-height: 1.6;
    color: var(--color-black);
    background-color: var(--color-white);
    overflow-x: hidden;
    -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
    -moz-osx-font-smoothing: grayscale;
}

/* ============================================
   КОНТЕЙНЕР
   ============================================ */
.container {
    max-width: var(--container-max-width);
    margin: 0 auto;
    padding: 0 var(--container-padding);
}

/* ============================================
   ТИПОГРАФИКА
   ============================================ */
h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6 {
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-weight: 700;
    line-height: 1.2;
    letter-spacing: -0.02em;
}

.section-label {
    font-family: var(--font-sans);
    font-size: 0.75rem;
    font-weight: 500;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.15em;
    color: var(--color-gray);
    margin-bottom: 2rem;
    display: block;
}

.section-title {
    font-size: clamp(2.5rem, 8vw, 6rem);
    font-weight: 900;
    line-height: 1.1;
    margin-bottom: 3rem;
    color: var(--color-black);
}

.section-intro {
    font-size: 1.25rem;
    line-height: 1.7;
    color: var(--color-gray-dark);
    max-width: 700px;
    margin-bottom: 4rem;
}

/* ============================================
   ГЛАВНЫЙ ЭКРАН / HERO SECTION
   ============================================ */
.hero {
    position: relative;
    width: 100%;
    height: 100vh;
    min-height: 600px;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    overflow: hidden;
    background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--color-white) 0%, var(--color-ivory) 100%);
}

.hero-background {
    position: absolute;
    top: 0;
    left: 0;
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    background: 
        radial-gradient(circle at 20% 50%, rgba(26, 26, 26, 0.03) 0%, transparent 50%),
        radial-gradient(circle at 80% 80%, rgba(26, 26, 26, 0.02) 0%, transparent 50%);
    z-index: 1;
}

.hero-geometric {
    position: absolute;
    top: 0;
    left: 0;
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    z-index: 1;
    opacity: 0.05;
    background-image: 
        linear-gradient(45deg, var(--color-black) 25%, transparent 25%),
        linear-gradient(-45deg, var(--color-black) 25%, transparent 25%),
        linear-gradient(45deg, transparent 75%, var(--color-black) 75%),
        linear-gradient(-45deg, transparent 75%, var(--color-black) 75%);
    background-size: 60px 60px;
    background-position: 0 0, 0 30px, 30px -30px, -30px 0px;
}

.hero-content {
    position: relative;
    z-index: 2;
    text-align: center;
    padding: 2rem;
}

.hero-year-range {
    font-family: var(--font-sans);
    font-size: 0.875rem;
    font-weight: 500;
    letter-spacing: 0.3em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: var(--color-gray);
    margin-bottom: 2rem;
    opacity: 0;
}

.hero-title {
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: clamp(3rem, 12vw, 10rem);
    font-weight: 900;
    line-height: 1.1;
    color: var(--color-black);
    margin-bottom: 2rem;
}

.hero-title-line {
    display: block;
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(30px);
}

.hero-subtitle {
    font-family: var(--font-sans);
    font-size: clamp(1rem, 2vw, 1.5rem);
    font-weight: 300;
    line-height: 1.6;
    color: var(--color-gray-dark);
    max-width: 600px;
    margin: 0 auto 4rem;
    opacity: 0;
}

.hero-scroll-hint {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 0.5rem;
    font-size: 0.75rem;
    font-weight: 500;
    letter-spacing: 0.15em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: var(--color-gray);
    opacity: 0;
    animation: scrollHint 2s ease-in-out infinite;
}

@keyframes scrollHint {
    0%, 100% { transform: translateY(0); opacity: 0.6; }
    50% { transform: translateY(10px); opacity: 1; }
}

/* ============================================
   КУРАТОРСКОЕ ВВЕДЕНИЕ / MANIFESTO
   ============================================ */
.manifesto {
    padding: var(--section-spacing) 0;
    background-color: var(--color-white);
}

.manifesto-content {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(300px, 1fr));
    gap: 4rem;
    max-width: 1200px;
}

.manifesto-text-block {
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(30px);
}

.manifesto-subtitle {
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: 1.5rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
    color: var(--color-black);
}

.manifesto-text {
    font-size: 1.125rem;
    line-height: 1.8;
    color: var(--color-gray-dark);
    margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
}

.manifesto-text:last-child {
    margin-bottom: 0;
}

/* ============================================
   ТАЙМЛАЙН / TIMELINE SECTION
   ============================================ */
.timeline {
    padding: var(--section-spacing) 0;
    background-color: var(--color-ivory);
}

.timeline-track {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 3rem;
    position: relative;
    padding-left: 4rem;
}

.timeline-track::before {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    left: 0;
    top: 0;
    bottom: 0;
    width: 2px;
    background: linear-gradient(to bottom, var(--color-black), transparent);
}

.timeline-item {
    position: relative;
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateX(-30px);
    padding-bottom: 2rem;
}

.timeline-item::before {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    left: -4.5rem;
    top: 0.5rem;
    width: 12px;
    height: 12px;
    border-radius: 50%;
    background-color: var(--color-black);
    border: 2px solid var(--color-white);
}

.timeline-year {
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: 2rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    color: var(--color-black);
    margin-bottom: 0.5rem;
}

.timeline-content h3 {
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: 1.5rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    margin-bottom: 0.5rem;
    color: var(--color-black);
}

.timeline-content p {
    font-size: 1rem;
    line-height: 1.7;
    color: var(--color-gray-dark);
}

/* ============================================
   РАЗДЕЛИТЕЛЬ С ЦИТАТОЙ
   ============================================ */
.quote-separator {
    padding: var(--section-spacing) 0;
    background-color: var(--color-black);
    color: var(--color-white);
}

.quote-text {
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: clamp(1.5rem, 4vw, 3rem);
    font-weight: 400;
    line-height: 1.4;
    font-style: italic;
    max-width: 900px;
    margin: 0 auto;
    text-align: center;
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(30px);
}

.quote-author {
    display: block;
    font-family: var(--font-sans);
    font-size: 1rem;
    font-weight: 500;
    font-style: normal;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.15em;
    margin-top: 2rem;
    text-align: center;
    opacity: 0.7;
}

/* ============================================
   СЕТКА ЛИЧНОСТЕЙ / PERSONALITIES GRID
   ============================================ */
.personalities {
    padding: var(--section-spacing) 0;
    background-color: var(--color-white);
}

.personalities-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(320px, 1fr));
    gap: 2rem;
    margin-top: 4rem;
}

/* ============================================
   КАРТОЧКА ЛИЧНОСТИ
   ============================================
   КАК ИЗМЕНИТЬ КАРТОЧКУ:
   - .personality-card-image-placeholder — замените на <img src="путь/к/изображению.jpg">
   - .personality-card-name — имя личности
   - .personality-card-country — страна
   - .personality-card-years — годы жизни
   - .personality-card-description — краткое описание
   - .personality-card-tag — тег/категория
   ============================================ */
.personality-card {
    position: relative;
    background-color: var(--color-white);
    border: 1px solid var(--color-gray-light);
    overflow: hidden;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: all 0.4s var(--ease-smooth);
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(30px);
}

.personality-card:hover {
    transform: translateY(-5px);
    box-shadow: 0 20px 60px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
    border-color: var(--color-black);
}

.personality-card-image {
    width: 100%;
    height: 400px;
    background-color: var(--color-gray-light);
    position: relative;
    overflow: hidden;
}

.personality-card-placeholder {
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: 1.5rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    color: var(--color-gray);
    background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--color-gray-light) 0%, var(--color-ivory) 100%);
}

/* Стили для изображений в карточках */
.personality-card-image img {
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    object-fit: cover;
    transition: transform 0.6s var(--ease-smooth);
}

.personality-card:hover .personality-card-image img {
    transform: scale(1.1);
}

.personality-card-content {
    padding: 2rem;
}

.personality-card-meta {
    display: flex;
    gap: 1rem;
    margin-bottom: 1rem;
    font-size: 0.75rem;
    font-weight: 500;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.1em;
    color: var(--color-gray);
}

.personality-card-name {
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: 1.75rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    margin-bottom: 0.75rem;
    color: var(--color-black);
}

.personality-card-description {
    font-size: 1rem;
    line-height: 1.6;
    color: var(--color-gray-dark);
    margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
}

.personality-card-tag {
    display: inline-block;
    font-size: 0.75rem;
    font-weight: 500;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.1em;
    padding: 0.5rem 1rem;
    background-color: var(--color-black);
    color: var(--color-white);
}

/* ============================================
   МОДАЛЬНОЕ ОКНО ДЕТАЛЕЙ
   ============================================ */
.personality-modal {
    position: fixed;
    top: 0;
    left: 0;
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    background-color: rgba(10, 10, 10, 0.95);
    z-index: 1000;
    display: none;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    padding: 2rem;
    overflow-y: auto;
    backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
}

.personality-modal.active {
    display: flex;
}

.modal-close {
    position: fixed;
    top: 2rem;
    right: 2rem;
    width: 48px;
    height: 48px;
    background-color: transparent;
    border: 1px solid var(--color-white);
    color: var(--color-white);
    cursor: pointer;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    transition: all 0.3s var(--ease-smooth);
    z-index: 1001;
}

.modal-close:hover {
    background-color: var(--color-white);
    color: var(--color-black);
}

.modal-content {
    max-width: 1200px;
    width: 100%;
    max-height: 90vh;
    overflow-y: auto;
    padding: 2rem 0;
}

/* ============================================
   ДЕТАЛЬНЫЙ ПРОФИЛЬ ЛИЧНОСТИ
   ============================================
   КАК ИЗМЕНИТЬ ПРОФИЛЬ:
   - .personality-detail-image-placeholder — замените на <img>
   - .personality-detail-name — имя
   - .personality-detail-country — страна
   - .personality-detail-years — годы
   - .personality-detail-section — биография, инновации, влияние
   - .personality-detail-gallery — галерея работ
   ============================================ */
.personality-detail {
    background-color: var(--color-white);
    color: var(--color-black);
    border-radius: 0;
}

.personality-detail-hero {
    width: 100%;
    height: 50vh;
    min-height: 400px;
    background-color: var(--color-gray-light);
    margin-bottom: 4rem;
    position: relative;
    overflow: hidden;
    border-radius: 0;
}

.personality-detail-image-placeholder {
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: 3rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    color: var(--color-gray);
    background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--color-gray-light) 0%, var(--color-ivory) 100%);
}

/* Стили для изображений в детальных профилях */
.personality-detail-hero img {
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    object-fit: cover;
}

.personality-detail-content {
    padding: 0 2rem 4rem;
}

.personality-detail-header {
    margin-bottom: 4rem;
    padding-bottom: 2.5rem;
    border-bottom: 2px solid var(--color-gray-light);
    position: relative;
}

.personality-detail-header::after {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    bottom: -2px;
    left: 0;
    width: 100px;
    height: 2px;
    background-color: var(--color-black);
}

.personality-detail-meta {
    display: flex;
    gap: 1.5rem;
    margin-bottom: 1rem;
    font-size: 0.875rem;
    font-weight: 500;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.1em;
    color: var(--color-gray);
}

.personality-detail-name {
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: clamp(2.5rem, 6vw, 4.5rem);
    font-weight: 900;
    line-height: 1.1;
    color: var(--color-black);
}

.personality-detail-body {
    max-width: 800px;
}

.personality-detail-section {
    margin-bottom: 4rem;
    padding: 2.5rem;
    background-color: var(--color-ivory);
    border: 1px solid var(--color-gray-light);
    transition: all 0.3s var(--ease-smooth);
    position: relative;
}

.personality-detail-section:hover {
    border-color: var(--color-black);
    box-shadow: 0 10px 40px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.05);
    transform: translateY(-2px);
}

.personality-detail-section::before {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    top: 0;
    left: 0;
    width: 4px;
    height: 100%;
    background-color: var(--color-black);
    opacity: 0;
    transition: opacity 0.3s var(--ease-smooth);
}

.personality-detail-section:hover::before {
    opacity: 1;
}

.personality-detail-section h3 {
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: 1.75rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
    color: var(--color-black);
}

.personality-detail-section p {
    font-size: 1.125rem;
    line-height: 1.8;
    color: var(--color-gray-dark);
    margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
}

.personality-detail-section ul {
    list-style: none;
    padding: 0;
}

.personality-detail-section li {
    font-size: 1.125rem;
    line-height: 1.8;
    color: var(--color-gray-dark);
    padding-left: 2rem;
    position: relative;
    margin-bottom: 1rem;
}

.personality-detail-section li::before {
    content: '—';
    position: absolute;
    left: 0;
    color: var(--color-black);
    font-weight: 700;
}

.personality-detail-gallery {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(250px, 1fr));
    gap: 2rem;
    margin-top: 4rem;
    padding-top: 4rem;
    border-top: 2px solid var(--color-gray-light);
    position: relative;
}

.personality-detail-gallery::before {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    top: -2px;
    left: 0;
    width: 100px;
    height: 2px;
    background-color: var(--color-black);
}

.gallery-item {
    width: 100%;
    height: 300px;
    background-color: var(--color-gray-light);
    position: relative;
    overflow: hidden;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: all 0.4s var(--ease-smooth);
    border: 1px solid var(--color-gray-light);
}

.gallery-item:hover {
    transform: translateY(-5px) scale(1.02);
    box-shadow: 0 15px 50px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.15);
    border-color: var(--color-black);
}

.gallery-placeholder {
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    font-family: var(--font-sans);
    font-size: 0.875rem;
    font-weight: 500;
    color: var(--color-gray);
    background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--color-gray-light) 0%, var(--color-ivory) 100%);
}

/* Стили для изображений в галерее */
.gallery-item img {
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    object-fit: cover;
    transition: transform 0.4s var(--ease-smooth);
}

.gallery-item:hover img {
    transform: scale(1.05);
}

/* ============================================
   ТЕМЫ И ВЛИЯНИЕ / THEMES SECTION
   ============================================ */
.themes {
    padding: var(--section-spacing) 0;
    background-color: var(--color-ivory);
}

.themes-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(300px, 1fr));
    gap: 2rem;
    margin-top: 4rem;
}

.theme-card {
    background-color: var(--color-white);
    padding: 3rem;
    border: 1px solid var(--color-gray-light);
    transition: all 0.4s var(--ease-smooth);
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(30px);
}

.theme-card:hover {
    transform: translateY(-5px);
    box-shadow: 0 20px 60px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
    border-color: var(--color-black);
}

.theme-title {
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: 1.5rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
    color: var(--color-black);
}

.theme-description {
    font-size: 1rem;
    line-height: 1.7;
    color: var(--color-gray-dark);
}

/* ============================================
   ЗАКЛЮЧЕНИЕ / CLOSING SECTION
   ============================================ */
.closing {
    padding: var(--section-spacing) 0;
    background-color: var(--color-white);
}

.closing-content {
    max-width: 900px;
    margin: 0 auto;
    text-align: center;
}

.closing-title {
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: clamp(2.5rem, 6vw, 4.5rem);
    font-weight: 900;
    margin-bottom: 3rem;
    color: var(--color-black);
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(30px);
}

.closing-text {
    font-size: 1.25rem;
    line-height: 1.8;
    color: var(--color-gray-dark);
}

.closing-text p {
    margin-bottom: 2rem;
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(30px);
}

.closing-text p:last-child {
    margin-bottom: 0;
}

/* ============================================
   ФУТЕР / FOOTER
   ============================================ */
.footer {
    padding: 4rem 0;
    background-color: var(--color-black);
    color: var(--color-white);
    text-align: center;
}

.footer-content {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 1rem;
}

.footer-text {
    font-size: 0.875rem;
    font-weight: 400;
    letter-spacing: 0.1em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    opacity: 0.7;
}

.footer-year {
    font-size: 0.75rem;
    font-weight: 300;
    opacity: 0.5;
}

/* ============================================
   АДАПТИВНОСТЬ / RESPONSIVE
   ============================================ */
@media (max-width: 1024px) {
    :root {
        --container-padding: 4vw;
        --section-spacing: 10vh;
    }
    
    .personalities-grid {
        grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(280px, 1fr));
        gap: 1.5rem;
    }
    
    .manifesto-content {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
        gap: 3rem;
    }
}

@media (max-width: 768px) {
    :root {
        --container-padding: 5vw;
        --section-spacing: 8vh;
    }
    
    .hero {
        min-height: 500px;
    }
    
    .personalities-grid {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }
    
    .themes-grid {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }
    
    .timeline-track {
        padding-left: 2rem;
    }
    
    .timeline-item::before {
        left: -2.5rem;
    }
    
    .personality-detail-hero {
        height: 40vh;
        min-height: 300px;
    }
    
    .personality-detail-content {
        padding: 0 1rem;
    }
    
    .modal-close {
        top: 1rem;
        right: 1rem;
    }
}

@media (max-width: 480px) {
    .hero-title {
        font-size: 3rem;
    }
    
    .section-title {
        font-size: 2rem;
    }
    
    .personality-card-image {
        height: 300px;
    }
}

/* ============================================
   УТИЛИТЫ ДЛЯ АНИМАЦИЙ
   ============================================ */
.fade-in {
    opacity: 1 !important;
    transform: translateY(0) !important;
}

/* ============================================
   ПОДДЕРЖКА REDUCED MOTION
   ============================================ */
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
    *,
    *::before,
    *::after {
        animation-duration: 0.01ms !important;
        animation-iteration-count: 1 !important;
        transition-duration: 0.01ms !important;
    }
    
    .hero-scroll-hint {
        animation: none;
    }
}


```

```css
/* ============================================
   ПРЕМИАЛЬНАЯ ВЫСТАВКА: АВАНГАРД В МОДЕ
   ============================================
   Стили для цифровой выставки об авангарде
   в искусстве и моде конца XIX–XX века
   ============================================ */

/* ============================================
   БАЗОВЫЕ СТИЛИ И СБРОС
   ============================================ */
* {
    margin: 0;
    padding: 0;
    box-sizing: border-box;
}

:root {
    /* Цветовая палитра */
    --color-black: #0a0a0a;
    --color-white: #fafafa;
    --color-ivory: #f5f5f0;
    --color-gray-light: #e8e8e3;
    --color-gray: #8a8a85;
    --color-gray-dark: #3a3a35;
    --color-accent: #1a1a1a;
    
    /* Типографика */
    --font-serif: 'Playfair Display', serif;
    --font-sans: 'Inter', sans-serif;
    
    /* Размеры */
    --container-max-width: 1400px;
    --container-padding: 5vw;
    --section-spacing: 15vh;
    
    /* Анимации */
    --ease-smooth: cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1);
    --ease-out-expo: cubic-bezier(0.19, 1, 0.22, 1);
}

html {
    font-size: 16px;
    /* scroll-behavior удалён для полностью нативной прокрутки без задержек */
}

body {
    font-family: var(--font-sans);
    font-weight: 400;
    line-height: 1.6;
    color: var(--color-black);
    background-color: var(--color-white);
    overflow-x: hidden;
    -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
    -moz-osx-font-smoothing: grayscale;
}

/* ============================================
   КОНТЕЙНЕР
   ============================================ */
.container {
    max-width: var(--container-max-width);
    margin: 0 auto;
    padding: 0 var(--container-padding);
}

/* ============================================
   ТИПОГРАФИКА
   ============================================ */
h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6 {
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-weight: 700;
    line-height: 1.2;
    letter-spacing: -0.02em;
}

.section-label {
    font-family: var(--font-sans);
    font-size: 0.75rem;
    font-weight: 500;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.15em;
    color: var(--color-gray);
    margin-bottom: 2rem;
    display: block;
}

.section-title {
    font-size: clamp(2.5rem, 8vw, 6rem);
    font-weight: 900;
    line-height: 1.1;
    margin-bottom: 3rem;
    color: var(--color-black);
}

.section-intro {
    font-size: 1.25rem;
    line-height: 1.7;
    color: var(--color-gray-dark);
    max-width: 700px;
    margin-bottom: 4rem;
}

/* ============================================
   ГЛАВНЫЙ ЭКРАН / HERO SECTION
   ============================================ */
.hero {
    position: relative;
    width: 100%;
    height: 100vh;
    min-height: 600px;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    overflow: hidden;
    background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--color-white) 0%, var(--color-ivory) 100%);
}

.hero-background {
    position: absolute;
    top: 0;
    left: 0;
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    background: 
        radial-gradient(circle at 20% 50%, rgba(26, 26, 26, 0.03) 0%, transparent 50%),
        radial-gradient(circle at 80% 80%, rgba(26, 26, 26, 0.02) 0%, transparent 50%);
    z-index: 1;
}

.hero-geometric {
    position: absolute;
    top: 0;
    left: 0;
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    z-index: 1;
    opacity: 0.05;
    background-image: 
        linear-gradient(45deg, var(--color-black) 25%, transparent 25%),
        linear-gradient(-45deg, var(--color-black) 25%, transparent 25%),
        linear-gradient(45deg, transparent 75%, var(--color-black) 75%),
        linear-gradient(-45deg, transparent 75%, var(--color-black) 75%);
    background-size: 60px 60px;
    background-position: 0 0, 0 30px, 30px -30px, -30px 0px;
}

.hero-content {
    position: relative;
    z-index: 2;
    text-align: center;
    padding: 2rem;
}

.hero-year-range {
    font-family: var(--font-sans);
    font-size: 0.875rem;
    font-weight: 500;
    letter-spacing: 0.3em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: var(--color-gray);
    margin-bottom: 2rem;
    opacity: 0;
}

.hero-title {
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: clamp(3rem, 12vw, 10rem);
    font-weight: 900;
    line-height: 1.1;
    color: var(--color-black);
    margin-bottom: 2rem;
}

.hero-title-line {
    display: block;
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(30px);
}

.hero-subtitle {
    font-family: var(--font-sans);
    font-size: clamp(1rem, 2vw, 1.5rem);
    font-weight: 300;
    line-height: 1.6;
    color: var(--color-gray-dark);
    max-width: 600px;
    margin: 0 auto 4rem;
    opacity: 0;
}

.hero-scroll-hint {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 0.5rem;
    font-size: 0.75rem;
    font-weight: 500;
    letter-spacing: 0.15em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: var(--color-gray);
    opacity: 0;
    animation: scrollHint 2s ease-in-out infinite;
}

@keyframes scrollHint {
    0%, 100% { transform: translateY(0); opacity: 0.6; }
    50% { transform: translateY(10px); opacity: 1; }
}

/* ============================================
   КУРАТОРСКОЕ ВВЕДЕНИЕ / MANIFESTO
   ============================================ */
.manifesto {
    padding: var(--section-spacing) 0;
    background-color: var(--color-white);
}

.manifesto-content {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(300px, 1fr));
    gap: 4rem;
    max-width: 1200px;
}

.manifesto-text-block {
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(30px);
}

.manifesto-subtitle {
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: 1.5rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
    color: var(--color-black);
}

.manifesto-text {
    font-size: 1.125rem;
    line-height: 1.8;
    color: var(--color-gray-dark);
    margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
}

.manifesto-text:last-child {
    margin-bottom: 0;
}

/* ============================================
   ТАЙМЛАЙН / TIMELINE SECTION
   ============================================ */
.timeline {
    padding: var(--section-spacing) 0;
    background-color: var(--color-ivory);
}

.timeline-track {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 3rem;
    position: relative;
    padding-left: 4rem;
}

.timeline-track::before {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    left: 0;
    top: 0;
    bottom: 0;
    width: 2px;
    background: linear-gradient(to bottom, var(--color-black), transparent);
}

.timeline-item {
    position: relative;
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateX(-30px);
    padding-bottom: 2rem;
}

.timeline-item::before {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    left: -4.5rem;
    top: 0.5rem;
    width: 12px;
    height: 12px;
    border-radius: 50%;
    background-color: var(--color-black);
    border: 2px solid var(--color-white);
}

.timeline-year {
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: 2rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    color: var(--color-black);
    margin-bottom: 0.5rem;
}

.timeline-content h3 {
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: 1.5rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    margin-bottom: 0.5rem;
    color: var(--color-black);
}

.timeline-content p {
    font-size: 1rem;
    line-height: 1.7;
    color: var(--color-gray-dark);
}

/* ============================================
   РАЗДЕЛИТЕЛЬ С ЦИТАТОЙ
   ============================================ */
.quote-separator {
    padding: var(--section-spacing) 0;
    background-color: var(--color-black);
    color: var(--color-white);
}

.quote-text {
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: clamp(1.5rem, 4vw, 3rem);
    font-weight: 400;
    line-height: 1.4;
    font-style: italic;
    max-width: 900px;
    margin: 0 auto;
    text-align: center;
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(30px);
}

.quote-author {
    display: block;
    font-family: var(--font-sans);
    font-size: 1rem;
    font-weight: 500;
    font-style: normal;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.15em;
    margin-top: 2rem;
    text-align: center;
    opacity: 0.7;
}

/* ============================================
   СЕТКА ЛИЧНОСТЕЙ / PERSONALITIES GRID
   ============================================ */
.personalities {
    padding: var(--section-spacing) 0;
    background-color: var(--color-white);
}

.personalities-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(320px, 1fr));
    gap: 2rem;
    margin-top: 4rem;
}

/* ============================================
   КАРТОЧКА ЛИЧНОСТИ
   ============================================
   КАК ИЗМЕНИТЬ КАРТОЧКУ:
   - .personality-card-image-placeholder — замените на <img src="путь/к/изображению.jpg">
   - .personality-card-name — имя личности
   - .personality-card-country — страна
   - .personality-card-years — годы жизни
   - .personality-card-description — краткое описание
   - .personality-card-tag — тег/категория
   ============================================ */
.personality-card {
    position: relative;
    background-color: var(--color-white);
    border: 1px solid var(--color-gray-light);
    overflow: hidden;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: all 0.4s var(--ease-smooth);
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(30px);
}

.personality-card:hover {
    transform: translateY(-5px);
    box-shadow: 0 20px 60px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
    border-color: var(--color-black);
}

.personality-card-image {
    width: 100%;
    height: 400px;
    background-color: var(--color-gray-light);
    position: relative;
    overflow: hidden;
}

.personality-card-placeholder {
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: 1.5rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    color: var(--color-gray);
    background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--color-gray-light) 0%, var(--color-ivory) 100%);
}

/* Стили для изображений в карточках */
.personality-card-image img {
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    object-fit: cover;
    transition: transform 0.6s var(--ease-smooth);
}

.personality-card:hover .personality-card-image img {
    transform: scale(1.1);
}

.personality-card-content {
    padding: 2rem;
}

.personality-card-meta {
    display: flex;
    gap: 1rem;
    margin-bottom: 1rem;
    font-size: 0.75rem;
    font-weight: 500;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.1em;
    color: var(--color-gray);
}

.personality-card-name {
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: 1.75rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    margin-bottom: 0.75rem;
    color: var(--color-black);
}

.personality-card-description {
    font-size: 1rem;
    line-height: 1.6;
    color: var(--color-gray-dark);
    margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
}

.personality-card-tag {
    display: inline-block;
    font-size: 0.75rem;
    font-weight: 500;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.1em;
    padding: 0.5rem 1rem;
    background-color: var(--color-black);
    color: var(--color-white);
}

/* ============================================
   МОДАЛЬНОЕ ОКНО ДЕТАЛЕЙ
   ============================================ */
.personality-modal {
    position: fixed;
    top: 0;
    left: 0;
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    background-color: rgba(10, 10, 10, 0.95);
    z-index: 1000;
    display: none;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    padding: 2rem;
    overflow-y: auto;
    backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
}

.personality-modal.active {
    display: flex;
}

.modal-close {
    position: fixed;
    top: 2rem;
    right: 2rem;
    width: 48px;
    height: 48px;
    background-color: transparent;
    border: 1px solid var(--color-white);
    color: var(--color-white);
    cursor: pointer;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    transition: all 0.3s var(--ease-smooth);
    z-index: 1001;
}

.modal-close:hover {
    background-color: var(--color-white);
    color: var(--color-black);
}

.modal-content {
    max-width: 1200px;
    width: 100%;
    max-height: 90vh;
    overflow-y: auto;
    padding: 2rem 0;
    scrollbar-width: thin;
    scrollbar-color: var(--color-gray) transparent;
}

.modal-content::-webkit-scrollbar {
    width: 8px;
}

.modal-content::-webkit-scrollbar-track {
    background: transparent;
}

.modal-content::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb {
    background-color: var(--color-gray);
    border-radius: 4px;
}

.modal-content::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb:hover {
    background-color: var(--color-gray-dark);
}

/* ============================================
   ДЕТАЛЬНЫЙ ПРОФИЛЬ ЛИЧНОСТИ
   ============================================
   КАК ИЗМЕНИТЬ ПРОФИЛЬ:
   - .personality-detail-image-placeholder — замените на <img>
   - .personality-detail-name — имя
   - .personality-detail-country — страна
   - .personality-detail-years — годы
   - .personality-detail-section — биография, инновации, влияние
   - .personality-detail-gallery — галерея работ
   ============================================ */
.personality-detail {
    background-color: var(--color-white);
    color: var(--color-black);
    border-radius: 0;
}

.personality-detail-hero {
    width: 100%;
    height: 50vh;
    min-height: 400px;
    background-color: var(--color-gray-light);
    margin-bottom: 4rem;
    position: relative;
    overflow: hidden;
    border-radius: 0;
}

.personality-detail-image-placeholder {
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: 3rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    color: var(--color-gray);
    background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--color-gray-light) 0%, var(--color-ivory) 100%);
}

/* Стили для изображений в детальных профилях */
.personality-detail-hero img {
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    object-fit: cover;
}

.personality-detail-content {
    padding: 0 2rem 4rem;
}

.personality-detail-header {
    margin-bottom: 4rem;
    padding-bottom: 2.5rem;
    border-bottom: 2px solid var(--color-gray-light);
    position: relative;
}

.personality-detail-header::after {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    bottom: -2px;
    left: 0;
    width: 100px;
    height: 2px;
    background-color: var(--color-black);
}

.personality-detail-meta {
    display: flex;
    gap: 1.5rem;
    margin-bottom: 1rem;
    font-size: 0.875rem;
    font-weight: 500;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.1em;
    color: var(--color-gray);
}

.personality-detail-name {
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: clamp(2.5rem, 6vw, 4.5rem);
    font-weight: 900;
    line-height: 1.1;
    color: var(--color-black);
}

.personality-detail-body {
    max-width: 800px;
}

.personality-detail-section {
    margin-bottom: 4rem;
    padding: 2.5rem;
    background-color: var(--color-ivory);
    border: 1px solid var(--color-gray-light);
    transition: all 0.3s var(--ease-smooth);
    position: relative;
}

.personality-detail-section:hover {
    border-color: var(--color-black);
    box-shadow: 0 10px 40px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.05);
    transform: translateY(-2px);
}

.personality-detail-section::before {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    top: 0;
    left: 0;
    width: 4px;
    height: 100%;
    background-color: var(--color-black);
    opacity: 0;
    transition: opacity 0.3s var(--ease-smooth);
}

.personality-detail-section:hover::before {
    opacity: 1;
}

.personality-detail-section h3 {
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: 1.75rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
    color: var(--color-black);
}

.personality-detail-section p {
    font-size: 1.125rem;
    line-height: 1.8;
    color: var(--color-gray-dark);
    margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
}

.personality-detail-section ul {
    list-style: none;
    padding: 0;
}

.personality-detail-section li {
    font-size: 1.125rem;
    line-height: 1.8;
    color: var(--color-gray-dark);
    padding-left: 2rem;
    position: relative;
    margin-bottom: 1rem;
}

.personality-detail-section li::before {
    content: '—';
    position: absolute;
    left: 0;
    color: var(--color-black);
    font-weight: 700;
}

.personality-detail-gallery {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(250px, 1fr));
    gap: 2rem;
    margin-top: 4rem;
    padding-top: 4rem;
    border-top: 2px solid var(--color-gray-light);
    position: relative;
}

.personality-detail-gallery::before {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    top: -2px;
    left: 0;
    width: 100px;
    height: 2px;
    background-color: var(--color-black);
}

.gallery-item {
    width: 100%;
    height: 300px;
    background-color: var(--color-gray-light);
    position: relative;
    overflow: hidden;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: all 0.4s var(--ease-smooth);
    border: 1px solid var(--color-gray-light);
}

.gallery-item:hover {
    transform: translateY(-5px) scale(1.02);
    box-shadow: 0 15px 50px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.15);
    border-color: var(--color-black);
}

.gallery-placeholder {
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    font-family: var(--font-sans);
    font-size: 0.875rem;
    font-weight: 500;
    color: var(--color-gray);
    background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--color-gray-light) 0%, var(--color-ivory) 100%);
}

/* Стили для изображений в галерее */
.gallery-item img {
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    object-fit: cover;
    transition: transform 0.4s var(--ease-smooth);
}

.gallery-item:hover img {
    transform: scale(1.05);
}

/* ============================================
   ТЕМЫ И ВЛИЯНИЕ / THEMES SECTION
   ============================================ */
.themes {
    padding: var(--section-spacing) 0;
    background-color: var(--color-ivory);
}

.themes-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(300px, 1fr));
    gap: 2rem;
    margin-top: 4rem;
}

.theme-card {
    background-color: var(--color-white);
    padding: 3rem;
    border: 1px solid var(--color-gray-light);
    transition: all 0.4s var(--ease-smooth);
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(30px);
}

.theme-card:hover {
    transform: translateY(-5px);
    box-shadow: 0 20px 60px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
    border-color: var(--color-black);
}

.theme-title {
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: 1.5rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
    color: var(--color-black);
}

.theme-description {
    font-size: 1rem;
    line-height: 1.7;
    color: var(--color-gray-dark);
}

/* ============================================
   ЗАКЛЮЧЕНИЕ / CLOSING SECTION
   ============================================ */
.closing {
    padding: var(--section-spacing) 0;
    background-color: var(--color-white);
}

.closing-content {
    max-width: 900px;
    margin: 0 auto;
    text-align: center;
}

.closing-title {
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: clamp(2.5rem, 6vw, 4.5rem);
    font-weight: 900;
    margin-bottom: 3rem;
    color: var(--color-black);
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(30px);
}

.closing-text {
    font-size: 1.25rem;
    line-height: 1.8;
    color: var(--color-gray-dark);
}

.closing-text p {
    margin-bottom: 2rem;
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(30px);
}

.closing-text p:last-child {
    margin-bottom: 0;
}

/* ============================================
   ФУТЕР / FOOTER
   ============================================ */
.footer {
    padding: 4rem 0;
    background-color: var(--color-black);
    color: var(--color-white);
    text-align: center;
}

.footer-content {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 1rem;
}

.footer-text {
    font-size: 0.875rem;
    font-weight: 400;
    letter-spacing: 0.1em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    opacity: 0.7;
}

.footer-year {
    font-size: 0.75rem;
    font-weight: 300;
    opacity: 0.5;
}

/* ============================================
   АДАПТИВНОСТЬ / RESPONSIVE
   ============================================ */
@media (max-width: 1024px) {
    :root {
        --container-padding: 4vw;
        --section-spacing: 10vh;
    }
    
    .personalities-grid {
        grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(280px, 1fr));
        gap: 1.5rem;
    }
    
    .manifesto-content {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
        gap: 3rem;
    }
}

@media (max-width: 768px) {
    :root {
        --container-padding: 5vw;
        --section-spacing: 8vh;
    }
    
    .hero {
        min-height: 500px;
    }
    
    .personalities-grid {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }
    
    .themes-grid {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }
    
    .timeline-track {
        padding-left: 2rem;
    }
    
    .timeline-item::before {
        left: -2.5rem;
    }
    
    .personality-detail-hero {
        height: 40vh;
        min-height: 300px;
    }
    
    .personality-detail-content {
        padding: 0 1rem;
    }
    
    .modal-close {
        top: 1rem;
        right: 1rem;
    }
}

@media (max-width: 480px) {
    .hero-title {
        font-size: 3rem;
    }
    
    .section-title {
        font-size: 2rem;
    }
    
    .personality-card-image {
        height: 300px;
    }
}

/* ============================================
   УТИЛИТЫ ДЛЯ АНИМАЦИЙ
   ============================================ */
.fade-in {
    opacity: 1 !important;
    transform: translateY(0) !important;
}

/* ============================================
   ПОДДЕРЖКА REDUCED MOTION
   ============================================ */
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
    *,
    *::before,
    *::after {
        animation-duration: 0.01ms !important;
        animation-iteration-count: 1 !important;
        transition-duration: 0.01ms !important;
    }
    
    .hero-scroll-hint {
        animation: none;
    }
}


```

Добавляю адаптивные стили для модального окна:


```css
/* ============================================
   ПРЕМИАЛЬНАЯ ВЫСТАВКА: АВАНГАРД В МОДЕ
   ============================================
   Стили для цифровой выставки об авангарде
   в искусстве и моде конца XIX–XX века
   ============================================ */

/* ============================================
   БАЗОВЫЕ СТИЛИ И СБРОС
   ============================================ */
* {
    margin: 0;
    padding: 0;
    box-sizing: border-box;
}

:root {
    /* Цветовая палитра */
    --color-black: #0a0a0a;
    --color-white: #fafafa;
    --color-ivory: #f5f5f0;
    --color-gray-light: #e8e8e3;
    --color-gray: #8a8a85;
    --color-gray-dark: #3a3a35;
    --color-accent: #1a1a1a;
    
    /* Типографика */
    --font-serif: 'Playfair Display', serif;
    --font-sans: 'Inter', sans-serif;
    
    /* Размеры */
    --container-max-width: 1400px;
    --container-padding: 5vw;
    --section-spacing: 15vh;
    
    /* Анимации */
    --ease-smooth: cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1);
    --ease-out-expo: cubic-bezier(0.19, 1, 0.22, 1);
}

html {
    font-size: 16px;
    /* scroll-behavior удалён для полностью нативной прокрутки без задержек */
}

body {
    font-family: var(--font-sans);
    font-weight: 400;
    line-height: 1.6;
    color: var(--color-black);
    background-color: var(--color-white);
    overflow-x: hidden;
    -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
    -moz-osx-font-smoothing: grayscale;
}

/* ============================================
   КОНТЕЙНЕР
   ============================================ */
.container {
    max-width: var(--container-max-width);
    margin: 0 auto;
    padding: 0 var(--container-padding);
}

/* ============================================
   ТИПОГРАФИКА
   ============================================ */
h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6 {
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-weight: 700;
    line-height: 1.2;
    letter-spacing: -0.02em;
}

.section-label {
    font-family: var(--font-sans);
    font-size: 0.75rem;
    font-weight: 500;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.15em;
    color: var(--color-gray);
    margin-bottom: 2rem;
    display: block;
}

.section-title {
    font-size: clamp(2.5rem, 8vw, 6rem);
    font-weight: 900;
    line-height: 1.1;
    margin-bottom: 3rem;
    color: var(--color-black);
}

.section-intro {
    font-size: 1.25rem;
    line-height: 1.7;
    color: var(--color-gray-dark);
    max-width: 700px;
    margin-bottom: 4rem;
}

/* ============================================
   ГЛАВНЫЙ ЭКРАН / HERO SECTION
   ============================================ */
.hero {
    position: relative;
    width: 100%;
    height: 100vh;
    min-height: 600px;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    overflow: hidden;
    background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--color-white) 0%, var(--color-ivory) 100%);
}

.hero-background {
    position: absolute;
    top: 0;
    left: 0;
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    background: 
        radial-gradient(circle at 20% 50%, rgba(26, 26, 26, 0.03) 0%, transparent 50%),
        radial-gradient(circle at 80% 80%, rgba(26, 26, 26, 0.02) 0%, transparent 50%);
    z-index: 1;
}

.hero-geometric {
    position: absolute;
    top: 0;
    left: 0;
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    z-index: 1;
    opacity: 0.05;
    background-image: 
        linear-gradient(45deg, var(--color-black) 25%, transparent 25%),
        linear-gradient(-45deg, var(--color-black) 25%, transparent 25%),
        linear-gradient(45deg, transparent 75%, var(--color-black) 75%),
        linear-gradient(-45deg, transparent 75%, var(--color-black) 75%);
    background-size: 60px 60px;
    background-position: 0 0, 0 30px, 30px -30px, -30px 0px;
}

.hero-content {
    position: relative;
    z-index: 2;
    text-align: center;
    padding: 2rem;
}

.hero-year-range {
    font-family: var(--font-sans);
    font-size: 0.875rem;
    font-weight: 500;
    letter-spacing: 0.3em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: var(--color-gray);
    margin-bottom: 2rem;
    opacity: 0;
}

.hero-title {
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: clamp(3rem, 12vw, 10rem);
    font-weight: 900;
    line-height: 1.1;
    color: var(--color-black);
    margin-bottom: 2rem;
}

.hero-title-line {
    display: block;
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(30px);
}

.hero-subtitle {
    font-family: var(--font-sans);
    font-size: clamp(1rem, 2vw, 1.5rem);
    font-weight: 300;
    line-height: 1.6;
    color: var(--color-gray-dark);
    max-width: 600px;
    margin: 0 auto 4rem;
    opacity: 0;
}

.hero-scroll-hint {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 0.5rem;
    font-size: 0.75rem;
    font-weight: 500;
    letter-spacing: 0.15em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: var(--color-gray);
    opacity: 0;
    animation: scrollHint 2s ease-in-out infinite;
}

@keyframes scrollHint {
    0%, 100% { transform: translateY(0); opacity: 0.6; }
    50% { transform: translateY(10px); opacity: 1; }
}

/* ============================================
   КУРАТОРСКОЕ ВВЕДЕНИЕ / MANIFESTO
   ============================================ */
.manifesto {
    padding: var(--section-spacing) 0;
    background-color: var(--color-white);
}

.manifesto-content {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(300px, 1fr));
    gap: 4rem;
    max-width: 1200px;
}

.manifesto-text-block {
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(30px);
}

.manifesto-subtitle {
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: 1.5rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
    color: var(--color-black);
}

.manifesto-text {
    font-size: 1.125rem;
    line-height: 1.8;
    color: var(--color-gray-dark);
    margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
}

.manifesto-text:last-child {
    margin-bottom: 0;
}

/* ============================================
   ТАЙМЛАЙН / TIMELINE SECTION
   ============================================ */
.timeline {
    padding: var(--section-spacing) 0;
    background-color: var(--color-ivory);
}

.timeline-track {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 3rem;
    position: relative;
    padding-left: 4rem;
}

.timeline-track::before {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    left: 0;
    top: 0;
    bottom: 0;
    width: 2px;
    background: linear-gradient(to bottom, var(--color-black), transparent);
}

.timeline-item {
    position: relative;
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateX(-30px);
    padding-bottom: 2rem;
}

.timeline-item::before {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    left: -4.5rem;
    top: 0.5rem;
    width: 12px;
    height: 12px;
    border-radius: 50%;
    background-color: var(--color-black);
    border: 2px solid var(--color-white);
}

.timeline-year {
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: 2rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    color: var(--color-black);
    margin-bottom: 0.5rem;
}

.timeline-content h3 {
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: 1.5rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    margin-bottom: 0.5rem;
    color: var(--color-black);
}

.timeline-content p {
    font-size: 1rem;
    line-height: 1.7;
    color: var(--color-gray-dark);
}

/* ============================================
   РАЗДЕЛИТЕЛЬ С ЦИТАТОЙ
   ============================================ */
.quote-separator {
    padding: var(--section-spacing) 0;
    background-color: var(--color-black);
    color: var(--color-white);
}

.quote-text {
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: clamp(1.5rem, 4vw, 3rem);
    font-weight: 400;
    line-height: 1.4;
    font-style: italic;
    max-width: 900px;
    margin: 0 auto;
    text-align: center;
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(30px);
}

.quote-author {
    display: block;
    font-family: var(--font-sans);
    font-size: 1rem;
    font-weight: 500;
    font-style: normal;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.15em;
    margin-top: 2rem;
    text-align: center;
    opacity: 0.7;
}

/* ============================================
   СЕТКА ЛИЧНОСТЕЙ / PERSONALITIES GRID
   ============================================ */
.personalities {
    padding: var(--section-spacing) 0;
    background-color: var(--color-white);
}

.personalities-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(320px, 1fr));
    gap: 2rem;
    margin-top: 4rem;
}

/* ============================================
   КАРТОЧКА ЛИЧНОСТИ
   ============================================
   КАК ИЗМЕНИТЬ КАРТОЧКУ:
   - .personality-card-image-placeholder — замените на <img src="путь/к/изображению.jpg">
   - .personality-card-name — имя личности
   - .personality-card-country — страна
   - .personality-card-years — годы жизни
   - .personality-card-description — краткое описание
   - .personality-card-tag — тег/категория
   ============================================ */
.personality-card {
    position: relative;
    background-color: var(--color-white);
    border: 1px solid var(--color-gray-light);
    overflow: hidden;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: all 0.4s var(--ease-smooth);
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(30px);
}

.personality-card:hover {
    transform: translateY(-5px);
    box-shadow: 0 20px 60px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
    border-color: var(--color-black);
}

.personality-card-image {
    width: 100%;
    height: 400px;
    background-color: var(--color-gray-light);
    position: relative;
    overflow: hidden;
}

.personality-card-placeholder {
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: 1.5rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    color: var(--color-gray);
    background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--color-gray-light) 0%, var(--color-ivory) 100%);
}

/* Стили для изображений в карточках */
.personality-card-image img {
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    object-fit: cover;
    transition: transform 0.6s var(--ease-smooth);
}

.personality-card:hover .personality-card-image img {
    transform: scale(1.1);
}

.personality-card-content {
    padding: 2rem;
}

.personality-card-meta {
    display: flex;
    gap: 1rem;
    margin-bottom: 1rem;
    font-size: 0.75rem;
    font-weight: 500;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.1em;
    color: var(--color-gray);
}

.personality-card-name {
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: 1.75rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    margin-bottom: 0.75rem;
    color: var(--color-black);
}

.personality-card-description {
    font-size: 1rem;
    line-height: 1.6;
    color: var(--color-gray-dark);
    margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
}

.personality-card-tag {
    display: inline-block;
    font-size: 0.75rem;
    font-weight: 500;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.1em;
    padding: 0.5rem 1rem;
    background-color: var(--color-black);
    color: var(--color-white);
}

/* ============================================
   МОДАЛЬНОЕ ОКНО ДЕТАЛЕЙ
   ============================================ */
.personality-modal {
    position: fixed;
    top: 0;
    left: 0;
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    background-color: rgba(10, 10, 10, 0.95);
    z-index: 1000;
    display: none;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    padding: 2rem;
    overflow-y: auto;
    backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
}

.personality-modal.active {
    display: flex;
}

.modal-close {
    position: fixed;
    top: 2rem;
    right: 2rem;
    width: 48px;
    height: 48px;
    background-color: transparent;
    border: 1px solid var(--color-white);
    color: var(--color-white);
    cursor: pointer;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    transition: all 0.3s var(--ease-smooth);
    z-index: 1001;
}

.modal-close:hover {
    background-color: var(--color-white);
    color: var(--color-black);
}

.modal-content {
    max-width: 1200px;
    width: 100%;
    max-height: 90vh;
    overflow-y: auto;
    padding: 2rem 0;
    scrollbar-width: thin;
    scrollbar-color: var(--color-gray) transparent;
}

.modal-content::-webkit-scrollbar {
    width: 8px;
}

.modal-content::-webkit-scrollbar-track {
    background: transparent;
}

.modal-content::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb {
    background-color: var(--color-gray);
    border-radius: 4px;
}

.modal-content::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb:hover {
    background-color: var(--color-gray-dark);
}

/* ============================================
   ДЕТАЛЬНЫЙ ПРОФИЛЬ ЛИЧНОСТИ
   ============================================
   КАК ИЗМЕНИТЬ ПРОФИЛЬ:
   - .personality-detail-image-placeholder — замените на <img>
   - .personality-detail-name — имя
   - .personality-detail-country — страна
   - .personality-detail-years — годы
   - .personality-detail-section — биография, инновации, влияние
   - .personality-detail-gallery — галерея работ
   ============================================ */
.personality-detail {
    background-color: var(--color-white);
    color: var(--color-black);
    border-radius: 0;
}

.personality-detail-hero {
    width: 100%;
    height: 50vh;
    min-height: 400px;
    background-color: var(--color-gray-light);
    margin-bottom: 4rem;
    position: relative;
    overflow: hidden;
    border-radius: 0;
}

.personality-detail-image-placeholder {
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: 3rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    color: var(--color-gray);
    background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--color-gray-light) 0%, var(--color-ivory) 100%);
}

/* Стили для изображений в детальных профилях */
.personality-detail-hero img {
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    object-fit: cover;
}

.personality-detail-content {
    padding: 0 2rem 4rem;
}

.personality-detail-header {
    margin-bottom: 4rem;
    padding-bottom: 2.5rem;
    border-bottom: 2px solid var(--color-gray-light);
    position: relative;
}

.personality-detail-header::after {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    bottom: -2px;
    left: 0;
    width: 100px;
    height: 2px;
    background-color: var(--color-black);
}

.personality-detail-meta {
    display: flex;
    gap: 1.5rem;
    margin-bottom: 1rem;
    font-size: 0.875rem;
    font-weight: 500;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.1em;
    color: var(--color-gray);
}

.personality-detail-name {
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: clamp(2.5rem, 6vw, 4.5rem);
    font-weight: 900;
    line-height: 1.1;
    color: var(--color-black);
}

.personality-detail-body {
    max-width: 800px;
}

.personality-detail-section {
    margin-bottom: 4rem;
    padding: 2.5rem;
    background-color: var(--color-ivory);
    border: 1px solid var(--color-gray-light);
    transition: all 0.3s var(--ease-smooth);
    position: relative;
}

.personality-detail-section:hover {
    border-color: var(--color-black);
    box-shadow: 0 10px 40px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.05);
    transform: translateY(-2px);
}

.personality-detail-section::before {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    top: 0;
    left: 0;
    width: 4px;
    height: 100%;
    background-color: var(--color-black);
    opacity: 0;
    transition: opacity 0.3s var(--ease-smooth);
}

.personality-detail-section:hover::before {
    opacity: 1;
}

.personality-detail-section h3 {
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: 1.75rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
    color: var(--color-black);
}

.personality-detail-section p {
    font-size: 1.125rem;
    line-height: 1.8;
    color: var(--color-gray-dark);
    margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
}

.personality-detail-section ul {
    list-style: none;
    padding: 0;
}

.personality-detail-section li {
    font-size: 1.125rem;
    line-height: 1.8;
    color: var(--color-gray-dark);
    padding-left: 2rem;
    position: relative;
    margin-bottom: 1rem;
}

.personality-detail-section li::before {
    content: '—';
    position: absolute;
    left: 0;
    color: var(--color-black);
    font-weight: 700;
}

.personality-detail-gallery {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(250px, 1fr));
    gap: 2rem;
    margin-top: 4rem;
    padding-top: 4rem;
    border-top: 2px solid var(--color-gray-light);
    position: relative;
}

.personality-detail-gallery::before {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    top: -2px;
    left: 0;
    width: 100px;
    height: 2px;
    background-color: var(--color-black);
}

.gallery-item {
    width: 100%;
    height: 300px;
    background-color: var(--color-gray-light);
    position: relative;
    overflow: hidden;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: all 0.4s var(--ease-smooth);
    border: 1px solid var(--color-gray-light);
}

.gallery-item:hover {
    transform: translateY(-5px) scale(1.02);
    box-shadow: 0 15px 50px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.15);
    border-color: var(--color-black);
}

.gallery-placeholder {
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    font-family: var(--font-sans);
    font-size: 0.875rem;
    font-weight: 500;
    color: var(--color-gray);
    background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--color-gray-light) 0%, var(--color-ivory) 100%);
}

/* Стили для изображений в галерее */
.gallery-item img {
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    object-fit: cover;
    transition: transform 0.4s var(--ease-smooth);
}

.gallery-item:hover img {
    transform: scale(1.05);
}

/* ============================================
   ТЕМЫ И ВЛИЯНИЕ / THEMES SECTION
   ============================================ */
.themes {
    padding: var(--section-spacing) 0;
    background-color: var(--color-ivory);
}

.themes-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(300px, 1fr));
    gap: 2rem;
    margin-top: 4rem;
}

.theme-card {
    background-color: var(--color-white);
    padding: 3rem;
    border: 1px solid var(--color-gray-light);
    transition: all 0.4s var(--ease-smooth);
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(30px);
}

.theme-card:hover {
    transform: translateY(-5px);
    box-shadow: 0 20px 60px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
    border-color: var(--color-black);
}

.theme-title {
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: 1.5rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
    color: var(--color-black);
}

.theme-description {
    font-size: 1rem;
    line-height: 1.7;
    color: var(--color-gray-dark);
}

/* ============================================
   ЗАКЛЮЧЕНИЕ / CLOSING SECTION
   ============================================ */
.closing {
    padding: var(--section-spacing) 0;
    background-color: var(--color-white);
}

.closing-content {
    max-width: 900px;
    margin: 0 auto;
    text-align: center;
}

.closing-title {
    font-family: var(--font-serif);
    font-size: clamp(2.5rem, 6vw, 4.5rem);
    font-weight: 900;
    margin-bottom: 3rem;
    color: var(--color-black);
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(30px);
}

.closing-text {
    font-size: 1.25rem;
    line-height: 1.8;
    color: var(--color-gray-dark);
}

.closing-text p {
    margin-bottom: 2rem;
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(30px);
}

.closing-text p:last-child {
    margin-bottom: 0;
}

/* ============================================
   ФУТЕР / FOOTER
   ============================================ */
.footer {
    padding: 4rem 0;
    background-color: var(--color-black);
    color: var(--color-white);
    text-align: center;
}

.footer-content {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 1rem;
}

.footer-text {
    font-size: 0.875rem;
    font-weight: 400;
    letter-spacing: 0.1em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    opacity: 0.7;
}

.footer-year {
    font-size: 0.75rem;
    font-weight: 300;
    opacity: 0.5;
}

/* ============================================
   АДАПТИВНОСТЬ / RESPONSIVE
   ============================================ */
@media (max-width: 1024px) {
    :root {
        --container-padding: 4vw;
        --section-spacing: 10vh;
    }
    
    .personalities-grid {
        grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(280px, 1fr));
        gap: 1.5rem;
    }
    
    .manifesto-content {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
        gap: 3rem;
    }
}

@media (max-width: 768px) {
    :root {
        --container-padding: 5vw;
        --section-spacing: 8vh;
    }
    
    .hero {
        min-height: 500px;
    }
    
    .personalities-grid {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }
    
    .themes-grid {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }
    
    .timeline-track {
        padding-left: 2rem;
    }
    
    .timeline-item::before {
        left: -2.5rem;
    }
    
    .personality-detail-hero {
        height: 40vh;
        min-height: 300px;
        margin-bottom: 2rem;
    }
    
    .personality-detail-content {
        padding: 0 1rem 2rem;
    }
    
    .personality-detail-header {
        margin-bottom: 2rem;
        padding-bottom: 1.5rem;
    }
    
    .personality-detail-section {
        margin-bottom: 2rem;
        padding: 1.5rem;
    }
    
    .personality-detail-gallery {
        margin-top: 2rem;
        padding-top: 2rem;
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }
    
    .modal-close {
        top: 1rem;
        right: 1rem;
        width: 40px;
        height: 40px;
    }
    
    .modal-content {
        padding: 1rem 0;
        max-height: 95vh;
    }
}

@media (max-width: 480px) {
    .hero-title {
        font-size: 3rem;
    }
    
    .section-title {
        font-size: 2rem;
    }
    
    .personality-card-image {
        height: 300px;
    }
}

/* ============================================
   УТИЛИТЫ ДЛЯ АНИМАЦИЙ
   ============================================ */
.fade-in {
    opacity: 1 !important;
    transform: translateY(0) !important;
}

/* ============================================
   ПОДДЕРЖКА REDUCED MOTION
   ============================================ */
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
    *,
    *::before,
    *::after {
        animation-duration: 0.01ms !important;
        animation-iteration-count: 1 !important;
        transition-duration: 0.01ms !important;
    }
    
    .hero-scroll-hint {
        animation: none;
    }
}


```

Добавляю визуальную обратную связь для кликабельных карточек:


```css
/* ============================================
   ПРЕМИАЛЬНАЯ ВЫСТАВКА: АВАНГАРД В МОДЕ
   ============================================
   Стили для цифровой выставки об авангарде
   в искусстве и моде конца XIX–XX века
   ============================================ */

/* ============================================
   БАЗОВЫЕ СТИЛИ И СБРОС
   ============================================ */
* {
    margin: 0;
    padding: 0;
```
